# Graph Report - himmel  (2026-11-08)

## Corpus Check
- 5188 files · ~9,593,866 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 1032 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: .jsonl 231, .out 135, .patch 106)

## Summary
- 50056 nodes · 95380 edges · 3414 communities (2277 shown, 1137 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 97% EXTRACTED · 3% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 3165 edges (avg confidence: 0.79)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `e84fb59e`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
